Parse "SELECT l_extendedprice * l_discount AS rev FROM '/mnt/work/src/data/client-check/lineitem.parquet' WHERE l_shipdate >= DATE '1994-01-01' AND l_shipdate < DATE '1995-01-01' AND l_discount BETWEEN 0.05 AND 0.07 AND l_quantity < 24\n"
Parse "COPY (SELECT l_extendedprice * l_discount AS rev FROM '/mnt/work/src/data/client-check/lineitem.parquet' WHERE l_shipdate >= DATE '1994-01-01' AND l_shipdate < DATE '1995-01-01' AND l_discount BETWEEN 0.05 AND 0.07 AND l_quantity < 24\n) TO STDOUT (FORMAT binary)"
Query 'ROLLBACK'
Parse "SELECT l_extendedprice * l_discount AS rev FROM '/mnt/work/src/data/client-check/lineitem.parquet' WHERE l_shipdate >= DATE '1994-01-01' AND l_shipdate < DATE '1995-01-01' AND l_discount BETWEEN 0.05 AND 0.07 AND l_quantity < 24\n"
